Algorithm for smooth local search:
```
def smooth_optimize(f0, time_budget, beta=10, ...):
    f = np.array positive normalized
    logf = np.log(np.maximum(f, tiny))
    best...
    for iters:
       f = exp(logf); f/=sum
       c = convolution(f,f)
       # compute score, update best
       check active max directly
       qc = logsumexp(beta*c)
       p = exp(beta*c - qc)/z
       grad = 2*corr(p,f)
       # center/scale grad: gmean = grad*sum? Use relative.
       # Multiplicative step: logf = logf - eta * grad / max(grad)
       # But Q gradient of sum=1 with Euclidean? Multiplicative not exact; use line search on actual score.
```

The LP iteration described:
- Normalize f sum=√(2n), M=||f*f||∞.
- Solve LP: maximize ∑ b_j subject to b≥0 and ||f*g||∞ ≤ M.
- Rescale g to sum √(2n), update f←(1-t)f+t g.
This is different from my LP min max. It maximizes sum of g given convolution with fixed f bounded by M. Since M fixed as current peak, the LP encourages g to be large where f*g constraints allow. It may increase sum while keeping f*g≤M, then blend. If we normalize, the new f may have lower peak and/or same sum. This is a **dual ascent** method.

In terms of score with sum fixed, it might be equivalent to solving an LP for a direction. Let's adapt.

Algorithm details:
Given f (nonnegative, sum normalized maybe 1). M = max(f*f). Solve LP:
maximize 1^T g
subject to (f*g)_k ≤ M for all k (linear in g; f fixed)
g ≥0.
No sum constraint! Then normalize g to sum=1. If the unconstrained optimum is finite, it will spread. This LP has variables g length n, constraints (2n-1), no equality. Objective positive; feasible. Because f nonnegative and M>0, there is feasible g. The solution g is a distribution (after normalization) that maximizes total mass allowed by the "capacity" f*g≤M. This is dual to covering problem. Then blend.

Let's implement LP matrix building efficiently. For given f length n, constraints A g ≤ M. A has rows k=0..2n-2, columns i=0..n-1, A[k,i]=f[k-i] if 0≤k-i<n else 0. This is a banded Toeplitz. Build dense using slicing:

```
A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n), dtype=float)
for k in range(2*n-1):
    lo = max(0, k-n+1)
    hi = min(n, k+1)
    # columns i=lo..hi-1, f索引 k-i from k-lo down to k-hi+1
    idx_f = k - np.arange(lo, hi)
    A[k, lo:hi] = f[idx_f]
```

```
   g /= sumg
   # line search
   alphas = [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.08,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005]
   best_alpha=0; best_cand=f; best_sc=score
   for alpha in alphas:
      cand=(1-alpha)*f+alpha*g
      sc=direct_score(cand)
      if sc<best_sc: update
   if best_alpha==0: # try random alpha? or break
      # maybe do small smooth step
      # We can perturb f and if improves use
      break/continue
   f=best_cand; score=best_sc
   if score<best_score: update global; stagnation=0 else stagnation++
   if stagnation>10: break
```

```
def convolve_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 1024:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=1
    Ntarget=2*n-1
    N=1 << (Ntarget-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    B=A*A
    return np.fft.irfft(B,N)[:2*n-1]
```

Projection simple:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond=u - cssv/ind >0
    if not np.any(cond): return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=ind[np.where(cond)[0]][-1]
    theta=cssv[rho-1]/rho
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0); return w/w.sum()
```
This might help if LP stalls. But code complexity; include maybe.

```
def make_starts(n, include_prev=False):
   starts=[]
   # central with tilts
   base=central(n)
   for eta in [-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5]:
      w=base*np.exp(eta*np.arange(n)/(n-1))

   # reversed tilts (same since reverse of tilt) maybe add -
```

Smooth search implementation details:
```
def smooth_improve(f, time_limit, global_best):
   f=normalize; best...
   beta=10.0
   last=time
   while time<last:
      c=conv(f,f)
      sc=2n maxc
      qc = logsumexp(beta*c) but beta*c may overflow if beta large; c~1e-3, beta=100 =>0.1 safe.
      p=softmax(beta*c)
      grad=2*corr_slice(p,f)
      # normalize grad to O(1)
      gscale = max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(grad)))
      for step in [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
         cand = project_simplex(f - step*grad/gscale)
         if score(cand)<best: update; break
   return best
```

Let's design `line_search_blend` efficiently:
```
def line_search_blend(f, g, current_score):
    n=len(f)
    best_score=current_score
    best_cand=f
    best_alpha=0.0
    alphas = [1.0,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.125,0.08,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01,0.006,0.003,0.001]
    for a in alphas:
       cand=(1.0-a)*f + a*g
       sc=direct_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc; best_cand=cand; best_alpha=a
    return best_cand,best_score,best_alpha
```
Computing cand inside loop allocates. Fine. We can stop if a too small? no.

`lp_loop` needs A, etc. It can update f. Must not modify A outside? It fills.

LP loop details with global_best:
```
def lp_loop(f, deadline, A, ones, c_obj, bounds, global_best, n):
    f=np.asarray(f,float).copy()
    f=normalize_sequence(f)
    score=direct_score(f); update_global(...)
    stagnation=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=np.convolve(f,f)
       M=float(np.max(c))
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: break
       fs=f/M
       # fill A
       for i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i]=fs
       # choose time
       rem=max(0.1, deadline - time.monotonic())
       lptime=min(1.5, max(0.3, rem*0.1))
       if linprog is None: break
       try res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=ones, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': lptime, 'max_iter': 100000, 'output_flag': 0})
       except Exception: break
       if res is None or res.x is None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       g=np.where(g>0, g, 0.0)
       sg=float(np.sum(g))
       if not np.isfinite(sg) or sg<1e-10: break
       g/=sg
       # maybe if g almost f, break?
       diff=float(np.sum(np.abs(g-f)))
       if diff<1e-7:
          stagnation+=1
          if stagnation>3: break
          continue
       cand, sc, alpha = line_search_blend(f,g,score)
       if sc < score - 1e-10 and alpha>0:
           f=cand; score=sc; update_global; stagnation=0
       else:
           stagnation+=1
           if stagnation>6: break
           # Try a small random step to escape
           if linprog is not None and time.monotonic()<deadline:
```

```
def resample_to_n(seq, n):
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
   m=len(arr)
   if m==0: return None
   if m==n: return arr
   # linear interpolation via np.interp
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   arr=np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr)
   return arr
```

```
def solve_lp_direction(A, b, c_obj, bounds, tlim):
    if linprog is None: return None
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': float(tlim), 'max_iter': 100000})
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
          return None
    if res is None or res.x is None: return None
    return np.asarray(res.x,float)
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def central_coefficients(n):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    return a


def normalize_sequence(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        if x.size == 0:
            return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    x /= s
    x[x < 0.0] = 0.0
    return x


def direct_score(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    f = f / s
    c = np.convolve(f, f)
    mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def update_global(global_best, score, f):
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < global_best[0]:
        global_best[0] = float(score)
        global_best[1] = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]


def resample_to_n(seq, n):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    if x.size == n:
        return x
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, x)


def solve_lp_direction(A, b, c_obj, bounds, tlim):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(tlim), "max_iter": 200000}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"max_iter": 200000}
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)


def line_search_blend(f, g, current_score):
    n = f.size
    best_score = current_score
    best_cand = f
    best_alpha = 0.0

    a = 1.0
    while a > 1.0e-5:
        cand = (1.0 - a) * f + a * g
        sc = direct_score(cand)
        if sc < best_score - 1.0e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_cand = cand
            best_alpha = a
            break
        a *= 0.5

    if best_alpha > 0.0:
        for factor in (1.3, 0.8):
            aa = best_alpha * factor
            if 0.0 < aa < 1.0:
                cand = (1.0 - aa) * f + aa * g
                sc = direct_score(cand)
                if sc < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_alpha = aa
    return best_cand, best_score, best_alpha


def lp_loop(f, deadline, A, ones, c_obj, bounds, global_best, n):
    f = normalize_sequence(f)
    score = direct_score(f)
    update_global(global_best, score, f)

    stag = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = np.convolve(f, f)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        fs = f / M
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = fs

        rem = max(0.1, float(deadline - time.monotonic()))
        tlim = min(1.8, max(0.35, 0.10 * rem))
        x = solve_lp_direction(A, ones, c_obj, bounds, tlim)

        if x is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1.0e-12:
            break
        g /= sg

        diff = float(np.sum(np.abs(g - f)))
        if diff < 1.0e-8:
            stag += 1
            if stag > 4:
                break
            continue

        cand, sc, alpha = line_search_blend(f, g, score)
        if sc < score - 1.0e-11 and alpha > 0.0:
            f = cand
            score = sc
            update_global(global_best, score, f)
            stag = 0
        else:
            stag += 1
            if stag > 6:
                break

    return f, score


def build_start_sequences(n, rng, include_previous=False):
    starts = []
    idx = 0

    def add(arr):
        nonlocal idx
        try:
            a = normalize_sequence(arr)
            sc = direct_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                starts.append((sc, idx, a))
        except Exception:
            pass
        idx += 1

    base = central_coefficients(n)
    denom = max(1, n - 1)
    xs = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    add(base)
    for t in (-0.45, -0.25, -0.12, 0.0, 0.12, 0.25, 0.45):
        add(base * np.exp(t * xs / denom))

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    add(np.exp(-0.35 * xs / denom))
    add(np.exp(0.35 * xs / denom))

    add(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 1.0e-4)
    add(np.concatenate([np.ones(n // 2 + 1), np.zeros(n - n // 2 - 1), np.ones(n // 2)]))

    if include_previous:
        try:
            prev = height_sequence_1
        except Exception:
            prev = None
        if prev is not None:
            r = resample_to_n(prev, n)
            if r is not None:
                add(r)
                rev = r[::-1]
                add(rev)
                mix = 0.5 * (r + r[::-1])
                add(mix)

    return starts


def run_lp_search(n, time_limit, global_best, start_f=None, seed=17):
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return
    if linprog is None:
        return

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    ones = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cap = 2.0 * float(n) + 1.0
    bounds = [(0.0, cap)] * n

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts = build_start_sequences(n, rng, include_previous=True)

    if start_f is not None:
        try:
            sf = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(start_f, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            if sf.size == n:
                sc = direct_score(sf)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    starts.append((sc, len(starts), sf))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not starts:
        return

    starts.sort()
    num_starts = 1
    if time_limit >= 400.0:
        num_starts = 2
    if time_limit >= 900.0:
        num_starts = 3
    num_starts = min(num_starts, len(starts))

    if num_starts == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif num_starts == 2:
        fracs = [0.72, 0.28]
    else:
        fracs = [0.52, 0.30, 0.18]

    for pos in range(num_starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 3.0:
            break
        item = starts[pos]
        sc0 = item[0]
        arr = item[2]
        t_for = float(time_limit) * fracs[pos]
        t_end = min(deadline - 3.0, time.monotonic() + t_for)
        if t_end <= time.monotonic() + 5.0:
            break
        _, score = lp_loop(arr, t_end, A, ones, c_obj, bounds, global_best, n)
        print("lp n={} start={} initial_score={:.6f} score={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(
            n, pos, sc0, score, global_best[0]), flush=True)


def random_local_search(f, time_limit, global_best, seed=99):
    f = normalize_sequence(f)
    n = f.size
    score = direct_score(f)
    update_global(global_best, score, f)

    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n == 0:
        return

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = f.copy()
        min_w = max(1, n // 250)
        max_w = max(min_w + 1, n // 16)
        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        stop = min(n, st + w)
        mult = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)))
        cand[st:stop] = cand[st:stop] * mult

        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
        sc = direct_score(cand)
        if sc < score - 1.0e-12:
            f = cand
            score = sc
            update_global(global_best, score, f)

    update_global(global_best, score, f)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    deadline = start_time + total_budget
    global_best = [np.inf, None]

    for n in (64, 128, 256):
        f = normalize_sequence(central_coefficients(n))
        sc = direct_score(f)
        update_global(global_best, sc, f)

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
        if global_best[1] is not None:
            return [float(x) for x in global_best[1]]
        f = normalize_sequence(central_coefficients(128))
        return [float(x) for x in f]

    # Quick fast search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        run_lp_search(256, min(90.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0), global_best, seed=101 + seed)

    # Medium search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        run_lp_search(512, min(420.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0), global_best, seed=202 + seed)

    # Main longer search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        run_lp_search(1024, max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0), global_best, seed=303 + seed)

    # Final polishing on the best length if time remains.
    if global_best[1] is not None:
        blen = len(global_best[1])
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0 and blen in (256, 512, 1024):
            run_lp_search(blen, min(120.0, rem - 30.0), global_best,
                          start_f=global_best[1], seed=404 + seed)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 40.0:
            random_local_search(global_best[1], min(35.0, rem - 3.0), global_best,
                                seed=505 + seed)

    if global_best[1] is None:
        f = normalize_sequence(central_coefficients(256))
        global_best[0] = direct_score(f)
        global_best[1] = [float(x) for x in f]

    final = np.asarray(global_best[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, None)
    final = normalize_sequence(final)

    # Final authoritative check when possible.
    try:
        authoritative = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
        if np.isfinite(authoritative):
            if not np.isfinite(global_best[0]) or authoritative < global_best[0]:
                global_best[0] = authoritative
        else:
            fb = normalize_sequence(central_coefficients(128))
            global_best[0] = direct_score(fb)
            global_best[1] = [float(x) for x in fb]
    except Exception:
        pass

    if global_best[1] is None:
        fb = normalize_sequence(central_coefficients(128))
        global_best[0] = direct_score(fb)
        global_best[1] = [float(x) for x in fb]

    return [float(x) for x in global_best[1]]
```
